## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

This is a competent investor update with a clear route: an agenda on slide 2, a highlights page, financial and operational tables, outlook, then appendices, so a reader can find what they need. Execution is disciplined, with consistent small-caps section labels, a shaded Q4 column that guides the eye in tables, and strong full-bleed factory photos on slides 14 to 21. The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts. The photo section interrupts the numbers without tying images to claims, and the appendix and footnotes are very small and faint, which suits a read-at-desk document but not a presented one.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the shaded current-period column in the tables (slides 4, 5, 7, 8), the dual-axis ASP versus margin chart on slide 25, the large clean photo pages, and the agenda with page numbers.

**Most useful repair:** Rewrite slide 3 with a few large headline numbers and a short summary, raise the contrast and size of body and footnote text, and replace label-only titles with sentences that state the point of each chart or table.

**What would change the score:** I would add direct data labels and takeaways to the key metrics charts on slides 22 to 24, which have tiny rotated axis labels and no callouts.

_Confidence high. 35 of 35 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a summary of Q4 and full-year 2022 results, framed around record revenue, operating income and net income, followed by a note on the macroeconomic outlook and the cost reduction focus. It then moves through a financial summary with quarterly and annual tables, an operational summary covering production, deliveries, storage and charging, and vehicle capacity by factory. Core technology and other highlights cover Full Self-Driving Beta, vehicle software, energy storage and solar. The outlook section gives 2023 volume guidance and the long-term growth target, and the back half holds photos of factories and products, key metrics charts, and full financial statements with GAAP to non-GAAP reconciliations. It closes with webcast details, definitions and forward-looking statement disclaimers. Slides worth studying include the summary highlights page, the quarterly financial table, and the key metrics charts that pair trailing twelve month trends across several measures on one page.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides are in the Tesla Q4 and FY 2022 update deck?**

The Tesla deck has 35 pages. Slide 2 lists its sections, including Highlights, Financial Summary, Operational Summary, Outlook, Key Metrics, Financial Statements and Additional Information.

**What does the Tesla deck say about 2022 revenue and net income?**

On slide 3, Tesla reports that total revenue grew 51% YoY to $81.5B in 2022. The same slide says net income (GAAP) more than doubled YoY to $12.6B.

**What does the Tesla deck say about energy storage deployments in 2022?**

Slide 11 says Tesla's energy storage deployments increased by 152% YoY in Q4 to 2.5 GWh, for a total deployment of 6.5 GWh in 2022. Slide 8 lists the 2022 storage deployed figure as 6,541 MWh.

**What volume outlook does the Tesla deck give for 2023?**

On slide 12, Tesla says it expects to remain ahead of the long-term 50% CAGR with around 1.8M cars for 2023. The same slide notes the company is planning to grow production as quickly as possible in alignment with that 50% CAGR target.

### Slide 3

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses short bolded metric lines grouped under Profitability, Cash and Operations, so the headline numbers can be scanned before the narrative text.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.
- Suggested repair: Rewrite slide 3 with a few large headline numbers and a short summary, raise the contrast and size of body and footnote text, and replace label-only titles with sentences that state the point of each chart or table.

**Text**

HIGHLIGHTS
SUMMARY
Profitability
16.8% operating margin in 2022; 16.0% in Q4
$13.7B GAAP operating income in 2022; $3.9B in Q4
$12.6B GAAP net income in 2022; $3.7B in Q4
$14.1B non-GAAP net income¹ in 2022; $4.1B in Q4
Q4-2022 was another record-breaking quarter and 2022 was another record-breaking year. In the last quarter, we achieved the highest-ever quarterly revenue, operating income and net income in our history. In 2022, total revenue grew 51% YoY to $81.5B and net income (GAAP) more than doubled YoY to $12.6B.
As we progress into 2023, we know that there are questions about the near-term impact of an uncertain macroeconomic environment, and in particular, with rising interest rates. The Tesla team is used to challenges, given the culture required to get the company to where it is today. In the near term we are accelerating our cost reduction roadmap and driving towards higher production rates, while staying focused on executing against the next phase of our roadmap.
Cash
Operating cash flow of $14.7B; free cash flow² of $7.6B in 2022
Operating cash flow of $3.3B; free cash flow of $1.4B in Q4
$1.1B increase in our cash and investments³ in Q4 to $22.2B
Our ASPs⁴ have generally been on a downward trajectory for many years. Improving affordability is necessary to become a multi-million vehicle producer. While ASPs halved between 2017 and 2022, our operating margin consistently improved from approximately negative 14% to positive 17% in the same period. This margin expansion was achieved through introduction of lower cost models, buildout of localized, more-efficient factories, vehicle cost reduction and operating leverage.
Operations
6.5 GWh of energy storage deployed in 2022, up 64% YoY
Record vehicle deliveries of 1.31 million in 2022
In any scenario, we are prepared for short-term uncertainty, while being focused on the long-term potential of autonomy, electrification and energy solutions. Our relentless cost control and cost innovation is why we believe that no other OEM is better equipped to navigate through 2023, and ultimately succeed in the long run, than we are.
(1) Excludes SBC (stock-based compensation).
(2) Free cash flow = operating cash flow less capex.
(3) Includes cash, cash equivalents and investments.
(4) ASP= average selling price.
TESLA

**Layout**

Single full-width data table on a white page with no title bar. The title block 'FINANCIAL SUMMARY' with '(Unaudited)' sits top-left at about 4.6% from the left edge and 9% from the top. The table spans roughly x 4.6% to 95.4%, with a label column on the left and six numeric columns (Q4-2021 to Q4-2022, then YoY) to the right; rows are stacked with uniform spacing and some blank gap rows between groups. Footnote sits bottom-left under the table, the page number '4' sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left, and a faint Tesla wordmark sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A data table, not a chart. Columns: label column '($ in millions, except percentages and per share data)', then Q4-2021, Q1-2022, Q2-2022, Q3-2022, Q4-2022 and YoY. Rows in order: Automotive revenues 15,967 / 16,861 / 14,602 / 18,692 / 21,307 / 33%; of which regulatory credits 314 / 679 / 344 / 286 / 467 / 49%; Automotive gross profit 4,882 / 5,539 / 4,081 / 5,212 / 5,522 / 13%; Automotive gross margin 30.6% / 32.9% / 27.9% / 27.9% / 25.9% / -466 bp; (blank gap); Total revenues 17,719 / 18,756 / 16,934 / 21,454 / 24,318 / 37%; Total gross profit 4,847 / 5,460 / 4,234 / 5,382 / 5,777 / 19%; Total GAAP gross margin 27.4% / 29.1% / 25.0% / 25.1% / 23.8% / -360 bp; (blank gap); Operating expenses 2,234 / 1,857 / 1,770 / 1,694 / 1,876 / -16%; Income from operations 2,613 / 3,603 / 2,464 / 3,688 / 3,901 / 49%; Operating margin 14.7% / 19.2% / 14.6% / 17.2% / 16.0% / 129 bp; (blank gap); Adjusted EBITDA 4,090 / 5,023 / 3,791 / 4,968 / 5,404 / 32%; Adjusted EBITDA margin 23.1% / 26.8% / 22.4% / 23.2% / 22.2% / -86 bp; (blank gap); Net income attributable to common stockholders (GAAP) 2,321 / 3,318 / 2,259 / 3,292 / 3,687 / 59%; Net income attributable to common stockholders (non-GAAP) 2,879 / 3,736 / 2,620 / 3,654 / 4,106 / 43%; (blank gap); EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (GAAP)(1) 0.68 / 0.95 / 0.65 / 0.95 / 1.07 / 57%; EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (non-GAAP)(1) 0.85 / 1.07 / 0.76 / 1.05 / 1.19 / 40%; (blank gap); Net cash provided by operating activities 4,585 / 3,995 / 2,351 / 5,100 / 3,278 / -29%; Capital expenditures (1,810) / (1,767) / (1,730) / (1,803) / (1,858) / 3%; Free cash flow 2,775 / 2,228 / 621 / 3,297 / 1,420 / -49%; Cash, cash equivalents and investments 17,707 / 18,013 / 18,915 / 21,107 / 22,185 / 25%. The Q4-2022 column is highlighted with a pale pink fill (about #F2DADB) spanning from header to last row, with the Q4-2022 values in black. Header row is a mid-grey band with white text. Row bands alternate light grey (#F2F2F2) and white. No chart, photo, logo or icon other than the small Tesla wordmark bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Minimal corporate financial-table style. White background. Titles and table labels in near-black; section title in dark grey #333333 with wide letter-spacing and all capitals. Header band mid-grey #7F7F7F with white text. Row bands #F2F2F2 alternating with white. Q4-2022 column pale pink highlight about #F2DADB. Footnote and page number in light grey (#BFBFBF and #BABABA). Typeface is a geometric sans-serif similar to Gotham (GothamSSm family): Medium for the section title and page number, Book for body and table text. Section title em 1.74% of slide height, body and table text em 1.79%, footnote em 1.35%, superscript footnote markers em 1.20%, page number em 1.58%. Thin rules absent; cells are separated only by band fills. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

TESLA

**Layout**

A single full-width financial table on a plain white slide. The title block FINANCIAL SUMMARY with (Unaudited) sits top-left at about 9 to 13 percent of slide height, left-aligned to the table's left edge at 4.6 percent. The table runs from 13.8 to 89.5 percent of height and from 4.6 to 95.4 percent of width, with a label column on the left and six numeric columns (2018 to 2022 and YoY) to the right, with blank spacer rows between groups. The footnote sits at the bottom left, the page number 5 at bottom left below the table margin, and a faint TESLA wordmark at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Table, unboxed, with a grey header row. Columns: label column (($ in millions, except percentages and per share data) in header); 2018; 2019; 2020; 2021; 2022 (highlighted pink); YoY. Rows in order: Automotive revenues 18,515 / 20,821 / 27,236 / 47,232 / 71,462 / 51%; of which regulatory credits 419 / 594 / 1,580 / 1,465 / 1,776 / 21%; Automotive gross profit 4,341 / 4,423 / 6,977 / 13,839 / 20,354 / 47%; Automotive gross margin 23.4% / 21.2% / 25.6% / 29.3% / 28.5% / -82 bp; blank spacer; Total revenues 21,461 / 24,578 / 31,536 / 53,823 / 81,462 / 51%; Total gross profit 4,042 / 4,069 / 6,630 / 13,606 / 20,853 / 53%; Total GAAP gross margin 18.8% / 16.6% / 21.0% / 25.3% / 25.6% / 32 bp; blank spacer; Operating expenses 4,430 / 4,138 / 4,636 / 7,083 / 7,197 / 2%; (Loss) income from operations (388) / (69) / 1,994 / 6,523 / 13,656 / 109%; Operating margin -1.8% / -0.3% / 6.3% / 12.1% / 16.8% / 464 bp; blank spacer; Adjusted EBITDA 2,395 / 2,985 / 5,817 / 11,621 / 19,186 / 65%; Adjusted EBITDA margin 11.2% / 12.1% / 18.4% / 21.6% / 23.6% / 196 bp; blank spacer; Net (loss) income attributable to common stockholders (GAAP) (976) / (862) / 721 / 5,519 / 12,556 / 128%; Net (loss) income attributable to common stockholders (non-GAAP) (227) / 36 / 2,455 / 7,640 / 14,116 / 85%; blank spacer; EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (GAAP) (0.38) / (0.33) / 0.21 / 1.63 / 3.62 / 122%; EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (non-GAAP) (0.09) / 0.01 / 0.75 / 2.26 / 4.07 / 80%; blank spacer; Net cash provided by operating activities 2,098 / 2,405 / 5,943 / 11,497 / 14,724 / 28%; Capital expenditures (2,101) / (1,327) / (3,157) / (6,482) / (7,158) / 10%; Free cash flow (3) / 1,078 / 2,786 / 5,015 / 7,566 / 51%; Cash, cash equivalents and investments 3,686 / 6,268 / 19,384 / 17,707 / 22,185 / 25%. **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Minimal white corporate slide. Background white #FFFFFF. Title FINANCIAL SUMMARY in widely letter-spaced dark grey #333333 geometric sans-serif (Gotham Medium style), about 1.7 percent of slide height for capitals. Subtitle (Unaudited) in the same family, about 1.7 percent. Table text in Gotham Book style sans-serif, black #000000, about 1.8 percent of slide height, right-aligned numbers and left-aligned labels. Header row fill #7F7F7F with white #FFFFFF text. Data rows alternate light grey #F2F2F2 bands with white gaps. The 2022 column is tinted light pink about #F2DCDD. Footnote text in light grey #BFBFBF about 1.35 percent. Page number in #BABABA. Wordmark in pale grey #C8C8C8. Lines are absent, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.

**Text**

FINANCIAL SUMMARY
Revenue
Total revenue grew 37% YoY in Q4 to $24.3B. YoY, revenue was impacted by the following items:
+ growth in vehicle deliveries
+ growth in other parts of the business
+ increase in ASP YoY (excluding FX impact), including favorable impact of model and regional mix
- negative FX impact of $1.4B¹
Profitability
Our operating income improved YoY to $3.9B in Q4, resulting in a 16.0% operating margin. YoY, operating income was primarily impacted by the following items:
+ growth in ASP (excluding FX) and vehicle deliveries (despite margin headwind from underutilization of new factories)
+ gross profit growth in other parts of the business
+ FSD revenue recognition of $324M in Automotive Sales²
+ lower stock-based compensation and payroll tax expense related to CEO awards
- higher raw material, commodity, logistics and warranty costs
- cost of production ramp of 4680 cells
- negative FX impact of over $300M¹
Cash
Quarter-end cash, cash equivalents and investments increased sequentially by $1.1B to $22.2B in Q4, driven mainly by free cash flow of $1.4B, partially offset by debt repayments of $497M.
(1) Impact is calculated on a constant currency basis. Actuals are compared against current results converted into USD using average exchange rates from Q4’21.
(2) This pertains to wide release of FSD Beta in the US and Canada during the period. Additionally, we expect to recognize nearly $1 billion of deferred revenue that remains for such customers over time as software updates are delivered.
6
TESLA

**Layout**

Single white page with a two-column text arrangement: category labels in a narrow left column starting at about 4.5% of slide width, and body text in a wider right column starting at about 19.3%. The title FINANCIAL SUMMARY sits top-left at about 9.3% from the top, with left margin about 4.6%. Three sections (Revenue, Profitability, Cash) run down the slide with generous vertical gaps, left-aligned. Footnotes sit bottom-left above the page number, which is at bottom-left in the margin; the faint Tesla wordmark sits bottom-right. No slide number other than the bottom-left '6'.

**Visuals**

none. The only non-text element is a small faint Tesla wordmark at bottom right, rendered as widely spaced capital letters T E S L A in light grey (#BFBFBF), about 2% of slide width tall-to-wide compact; it is an unboxed logo with no overlay.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, image or pattern. Title is GothamSSm Medium, all caps, letter-spaced widely, about 1.74% of slide height, colour #333333. Section labels are GothamSSm Book, about 1.79% of slide height, colour #111111. Body prose is GothamSSm Book about 1.79% of slide height in grey #6E6E6E; bullet lines are GothamSSm Book at the same size in black #000000 with + and - prefixes. Footnotes are GothamSSm Book about 1.35% of slide height in light grey #BFBFBF, with superscript markers (1) and (2) at about 0.9%. Page number is GothamSSm Medium about 1.58% in #BABABA. No lines, shapes, corner radii or shadows. Text is sans-serif, light-weight and generously spaced, with bullet lines spaced about 2.6% of slide height apart.

**Text**

OPERATIONAL SUMMARY
(Unaudited)

	2018	2019	2020	2021	2022	YoY
Model S/X production	101,553	62,931	54,805	24,390	71,177	192%
Model 3/Y production	152,977	302,301	454,932	906,032	1,298,434	43%
Total production	254,530	365,232	509,737	930,422	1,369,611	47%
Model S/X deliveries	99,451	66,771	57,085	24,980	66,705	167%
Model 3/Y deliveries	146,055	300,885	442,562	911,242	1,247,146	37%
Total deliveries	245,506	367,656	499,647	936,222	1,313,851	40%
of which subject to operating lease accounting	11,034	25,439	34,470	60,912	47,582	-22%
Total end of year operating lease vehicle count	37,134	49,901	72,089	120,342	140,667	17%
Global vehicle inventory (days of supply)(1)	22	13	15	6	16	167%
Solar deployed (MW)	326	173	205	345	348	1%
Storage deployed (MWh)	1,041	1,651	3,022	3,992	6,541	64%
Store and service locations	378	433	523	644	764	19%
Mobile service fleet(2)	411	758	894	1,281	1,584	24%

Supercharger connectors	12,002	16,104	23,277	31,498	42,419	35%
(1)Days of supply is calculated by dividing new car ending inventory by the relevant year’s deliveries and using 300 trading days (aligned with Automotive News definition).
(2)In 2021, we began including mobile service vehicles dedicated to tire repair in our mobile service fleet total. Prior period totals have been adjusted to reflect this change.

TESLA

**Layout**

Single full-width data table on a white slide with a small tracked-caps section heading top-left above the table, left-aligned to a shared margin at about 4.6% of slide width. The table spans from about 4.6% to 95.4% horizontally and from 14% to 76% vertically, with no title bar. Two footnotes sit in the lower left above the page number, and a faint TESLA wordmark sits bottom right; the page number '8' sits bottom left at about 3% x, 95% y.

**Visuals**

One table, not a chart. Columns: row label column (no header text), 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, YoY. Header row is dark grey (#7F7F7F) with white year labels. Rows, in order with values as printed: Model S/X production 101,553 / 62,931 / 54,805 / 24,390 / 71,177 / 192%. Model 3/Y production 152,977 / 302,301 / 454,932 / 906,032 / 1,298,434 / 43%. Total production (bold) 254,530 / 365,232 / 509,737 / 930,422 / 1,369,611 / 47%. Model S/X deliveries 99,451 / 66,771 / 57,085 / 24,980 / 66,705 / 167%. Model 3/Y deliveries 146,055 / 300,885 / 442,562 / 911,242 / 1,247,146 / 37%. Total deliveries (bold) 245,506 / 367,656 / 499,647 / 936,222 / 1,313,851 / 40%. Indented 'of which subject to operating lease accounting' 11,034 / 25,439 / 34,470 / 60,912 / 47,582 / -22%. Total end of year operating lease vehicle count 37,134 / 49,901 / 72,089 / 120,342 / 140,667 / 17%. Global vehicle inventory (days of supply)(1) 22 / 13 / 15 / 6 / 16 / 167%. Solar deployed (MW) 326 / 173 / 205 / 345 / 348 / 1%. Storage deployed (MWh) 1,041 / 1,651 / 3,022 / 3,992 / 6,541 / 64%. Store and service locations 378 / 433 / 523 / 644 / 764 / 19%. Mobile service fleet(2) 411 / 758 / 894 / 1,281 / 1,584 / 24%. Supercharger stations 1,421 / 1,821 / 2,564 / 3,476 / 4,678 / 35%. Supercharger connectors 12,002 / 16,104 / 23,277 / 31,498 / 42,419 / 35%. Highlight: the 2022 column cells are filled with a pale pink (about #F2D7D7) against the grey-and-white row bands. Row bands alternate light grey (#F2F2F2) with white gaps. Thin white horizontal separators between rows. Logo: a faint TESLA wordmark in light grey (about #D9D9D9), bottom right, letters widely spaced. No photographs, icons or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients, shadows or rounded corners. Section heading in dark grey #333333, tracked-out capitals, GothamSSm-Medium style, sans-serif, about 1.7% of slide height. Table text black #000000 in GothamSSm-Book sans-serif, about 1.8% of slide height, regular weight; bold for total rows. Header text white on #7F7F7F. Footnotes light grey #BFBFBF at about 1.35% of slide height. Page number #BABABA in GothamSSm-Medium at about 1.6%. Rectangular cell fills with no borders apart from thin white gaps.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places installed capacity by factory in one table with a clear status column, then pairs it with a regional market share chart.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.

**Text**

VEHICLE CAPACITY
In Q4, each of our vehicle factories produced a record number of vehicles. We
continued a gradual shift towards a more even regional mix of both production and
deliveries. While in Q2-2022 the 3rd month of the quarter accounted for 74% of
vehicle deliveries, that number fell to 64% in Q3, and to 51% in Q4. We are working
to reduce the percentage of vehicles delivered in the 3rd month and smooth
deliveries throughout the quarter, which will help to reduce cost per vehicle.
US: California, Nevada and Texas
The Model Y production line in Austin produced over 3,000 vehicles in a week
towards the end of Q4. In Q4, we produced enough in-house 4680 cells in a single
week to make over 1,000 battery packs. Pilot production of Tesla Semi commenced
in Q4 with first deliveries occurring in December 2022.
China: Shanghai
Production and delivery challenges in 2022 were largely concentrated in China.
Since our Shanghai factory has been successfully running near full capacity for
several months, we do not expect meaningful sequential volume increases in the
near term. Gigafactory Shanghai remains our main export hub, supplying vehicles to
most markets outside of North America.
Europe: Berlin-Brandenburg
The Model Y production line in Germany produced over 3,000 vehicles in a week
towards the end of Q4. We also introduced two new multi-layer paint colors from
our advanced paint shop at our Berlin factory – Quicksilver and Midnight Cherry Red.
Installed Annual Vehicle Capacity
Region Model Capacity Status
California Model S / Model X 100,000 Production
 Model 3 / Model Y 550,000 Production
Shanghai Model 3 / Model Y >750,000 Production

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page. The title VEHICLE CAPACITY sits top-left at about 9.3% from the top with a left margin of about 4.6%. The left column (about x 4.6% to 50.5%) holds one intro paragraph and three regional sections (US, China, Europe), each with a bold-style heading and a hairline divider above it. The right column (about x 54.6% to 95.7%) holds a centred table caption, the installed-capacity table, a light grey footnote, and a market-share line chart with its title and source line below. The page number 9 sits bottom-left at about x 3%, y 95%; the Tesla wordmark sits bottom-right beside the source line.

**Visuals**

Table: titled Installed Annual Vehicle Capacity, four columns (Region, Model, Capacity, Status) and nine body rows on a grey header. Rows: California | Model S / Model X | 100,000 | Production; (blank region) | Model 3 / Model Y | 550,000 | Production; Shanghai | Model 3 / Model Y | >750,000 | Production; Berlin | Model Y | >250,000 | Production; Texas | Model Y | >250,000 | Production; (blank) | Cybertruck | - | Tooling; Nevada | Tesla Semi | - | Pilot production; TBD | Roadster | - | In development; (blank) | Robotaxi & Others | - | In development. Header fill #7F7F7F with white text; body rows #F2F2F2 with thin white column dividers. Line chart: type line chart, title Market share of Tesla vehicles by region (TTM). Y axis 0% to 4% in 1% steps, unit %, light grey gridlines #D9D9D9. X axis quarterly from Q4 2016 to Q4 2022, 25 labels rotated about 45 degrees. Three series, no data labels printed, so values are about: US/Canada (blue) about 0.2, 0.2, 0.22, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.1, 1.25, 1.15, 1.05, 1.1, 1.1, 1.2, 1.4, 1.55, 1.65, 1.9, 2.3, 2.65, 3.15, 3.4, 3.6 percent; Europe (red) about 0.1, 0.15, 0.15, 0.17, 0.18, 0.19, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.7, 0.75, 0.8, 0.8, 0.85, 0.9, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5, 1.8, 1.8, 1.9, 2.2 percent; China (green) about 0.08 to 0.1 through 2017 and 2018, rising to about 1.85 by Q3 and Q4 2022. Legend top-left of plot: US/Canada, Europe, China with coloured line samples. No points highlighted. Table fill and chart colours: blue #4F81BD, red #C00000, green #92D050. Logo: Tesla wordmark in widely spaced light grey capitals at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in letter-spaced uppercase Gotham-style geometric sans, Medium weight, colour #333333, about 1.7% of slide height. Body text in Gotham Book, regular weight, colour #6E6E6E, about 1.8% of slide height, with generous line spacing. Section headings in Gotham Book, colour #111111, same size as body. Table caption and table body in Gotham Book #111111 and #000000; Model column in a heavier Medium-style weight; table header white on #7F7F7F. Footnote and source in Gotham Book, light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.3% of slide height. Chart title in Gotham Medium #7F7F7F; axis labels in Gotham Book #595959. Accents: blue #4F81BD, red #C00000, green #92D050. Hairline dividers in light grey #D9D9D9. Square corners, no shadows, no rounded shapes.

We are working / to reduce the percentage of vehicles delivered in the 3rd month and smooth / deliveries throughout the quarter, which will help to reduce cost per vehicle. ### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.

**Text**

CORE TECHNOLOGY
Autopilot and Full Self-Driving (FSD)
We have now released FSD Beta to nearly all customers in the US and Canada who bought FSD (approximately 400,000). This is an important milestone for our company. Every customer in the US and Canada can now access FSD Beta functionality upon purchase/subscription and start experiencing the evolution of AI-powered autonomy.
Vehicle Software
This year, our annual holiday release was the biggest ever. Our customers can now videoconference directly on their touchscreen via Zoom, streaming video from the car’s interior camera – an industry first. We integrated Apple Music into the media player, and added a live view of the interior camera to the mobile app when Dog Mode or Sentry Mode is active. In the latest Model S and X, you can now play all the games in your Steam library using the powerful built-in gaming computer, picking up where you left off on other devices, using wireless Bluetooth or wired USB game controllers.
Battery, Powertrain & Manufacturing
We are particularly focused on vehicle cost during this period of macroeconomic uncertainty, high interest rates (thus higher cost of vehicle financing) and vehicle price deflation. We continue to focus on cost efficiencies while improving functionality and reliability. While cost efficient manufacturing of EVs is still rare across most of the industry, it is critical for profitability at scale and will ultimately determine long-term success of OEMs.
Cumulative miles driven with FSD Beta (millions)
Miles driven per accident (millions)
Source: https://www.tesla.com/vehiclesafetyreport
10

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white page: a left text column (about x 4.6 to 51 percent) holding three headed sections separated by two thin grey horizontal rules, and a right column (about x 54 to 97 percent) holding a line chart on top and a clustered bar chart below. The section label CORE TECHNOLOGY sits top-left at about y 9 percent, left-aligned to the same margin as the body text at x 4.6 percent. The source line sits under the bar chart at the bottom right, the page number 10 sits bottom left at x 3 percent, and a faint TESLA wordmark sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two charts. (1) Line chart titled Cumulative miles driven with FSD Beta (millions). Y axis: Cumulative miles, millions, range 0 to 100, gridlines every 10, labels 0 to 100. X axis: monthly categories from Mar-21 to Dec-22 (22 labels, rotated about 35 degrees). One series drawn as a thick red line (#C0504D, about 4 px stroke, rounded joins). Approximate values, none printed on the slide: Mar-21 about 0; flat near 0 to about 1 through Sep-21; Oct-21 about 2; Nov-21 about 4; Dec-21 about 7; Jan-22 about 12; Feb-22 about 15; Mar-22 about 19; Apr-22 about 23; May-22 about 27; Jun-22 about 35; Jul-22 about 42; Aug-22 about 48; Sep-22 about 57; Oct-22 about 69; Nov-22 about 80; Dec-22 about 90 (line ends near the 90 gridline). No legend, no highlighted points. (2) Clustered vertical bar chart titled Miles driven per accident (millions), with a legend at top-left inside the plot: dark blue square #1F497D Tesla vehicles using Autopilot technology; mid blue square #4F81BD Tesla vehicles not using Autopilot technology; light grey square #BFBFBF United States average. Y axis: 0 to 7, integer gridlines, no unit label beyond the axis title. X axis: quarters grouped by year, Q3 Q4 Q1 Q2 Q3 Q4 for 2018 and 2019 (Q3 and Q4 2018 only, then Q1 to Q4 2019), Q1 to Q4 2020, Q1 to Q4 2021, Q1 to Q3 2022, with year labels 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022 beneath in a bracket with vertical separators. Estimated values, not printed on the slide (about): Q3 2018 Autopilot about 3.4, not-Autopilot about 1.9, US average about 0.5; Q4 2018 about 2.9, 1.2, 0.5; Q1 2019 about 2.9, 1.2, 0.5; Q2 2019 about 3.3, 1.4, 0.5; Q3 2019 about 3.9, 1.6, 0.5; Q4 2019 about 3.1, 1.5, 0.5; Q1 2020 about 4.9, 1.5, 0.6; Q2 2020 about 5.1, 1.6, 0.6; Q3 2020 about 5.1, 1.8, 0.6; Q4 2020 about 3.8, 1.3, 0.6; Q1 2021 about 4.7, 1.0, 0.7; Q2 2021 about 5.0, 1.4, 0.7; Q3 2021 about 5.6, 1.6, 0.7; Q4 2021 about 4.4, 1.5, 0.7; Q1 2022 about 6.6, 1.2, 0.7; Q2 2022 about 5.1, 1.5, 0.7; Q3 2022 about 6.3, 1.7, 0.7. No highlighted points. Flat graphic style, no photographs, no icons apart from the faint wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background (#FFFFFF). Section label CORE TECHNOLOGY in charcoal #333333, widely letter-spaced capitals, Gotham-style geometric sans-serif, medium weight, about 1.7 percent of slide height. Section headings in near-black #111111, Gotham Book sans-serif, about 1.8 percent of slide height. Body text in mid grey #6E6E6E, Gotham Book sans-serif, regular weight, about 1.8 percent of slide height, generous line spacing (about 1.5). Chart titles in grey #7E7E7E, Gotham Medium, about 1.8 percent. Chart tick labels in #595959, Gotham Book, about 1.35 percent. Source line in light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.35 percent. Page number in #BABABA, Gotham Medium, about 1.6 percent. Accent colours: red #C0504D for the line, dark blue #1F497D and mid blue #4F81BD for bars, light grey #BFBFBF for US average bars and rules. Gridlines in light grey #D9D9D9, hairline 0.75 pt. Divider rules in #BFBFBF, 0.75 pt, full width of the text column. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.

**Text**

OTHER HIGHLIGHTS
Energy Storage
Energy storage deployments increased by 152% YoY in Q4 to 2.5 GWh, for a total deployment of 6.5 GWh in 2022, by far the highest level of deployments we have achieved. Demand for our storage products remains in excess of our ability to supply. We are in the process of ramping production at our dedicated 40 GWh Megapack factory in Lathrop, California to address the growing demand. This factory should help to further accelerate growth of energy storage deployments.
Solar
Solar deployments increased by 18% YoY in Q4 to 100 MW, one of the strongest quarters in recent years. Despite supply chain challenges, we deployed 348 MW of solar in 2022, the highest deployment since 2017. Our solar installation team continues to improve installation efficiency, enabling higher volumes and stronger economics.
Services and Other
Both revenue and gross profit from Services and Other reached an all-time high in 2022. Much of the profit in 2022 was driven by used cars and transactions in support of a growing Tesla fleet, such as part sales, paid supercharging and others. While our used car ASPs declined sequentially in Q4 (which was reflective of the industry), our used car margins remained healthy.

**Layout**

Two columns on a white page: the left column (about 4.5% to 51% of width) holds three stacked text sections (Energy Storage, Solar, Services and Other) separated by two thin horizontal rules; the right column (about 54% to 96%) holds two stacked bar charts, the upper one for energy storage and the lower one for supercharging locations. The title OTHER HIGHLIGHTS sits top-left at about 9% from the top, left-aligned to the left margin. The page number 11 sits bottom-left at about 95% from the top, and a faint TESLA wordmark sits bottom-right on the same baseline.

**Visuals**

Chart 1: vertical bar chart titled Energy Storage deployments (GWh) placed below the top-right area. Y-axis 0 to 7 in steps of 1 with light grey horizontal gridlines, no unit label beyond the title (GWh). X-axis years 2015 to 2022. Bars in navy #1F497D for 2015 to 2021 and red #C0504D for 2022 (highlighted). Values not printed on the chart; about 0 for 2015 (barely visible), about 0.2 for 2016, about 0.4 for 2017, about 1.05 for 2018, about 1.65 for 2019, about 3.0 for 2020, about 4.0 for 2021, about 6.5 for 2022 (the text states 6.5 GWh in 2022). No legend. Chart 2: vertical bar chart titled Number of Supercharging locations placed below chart 1. Y-axis 0 to 5,000 in steps of 500 with light grey gridlines; X-axis years 2015 to 2022. Bars navy #1F497D for 2015 to 2021 and red #C0504D for 2022 (highlighted). Values not printed; about 590 for 2015, about 800 for 2016, about 1,130 for 2017, about 1,420 for 2018, about 1,830 for 2019, about 2,570 for 2020, about 3,480 for 2021, about 4,670 for 2022. No legend. Both charts are flat, with no borders, no shadows and no 3D effects. The TESLA wordmark is a small, widely letter-spaced light grey logo at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate investor-presentation style. Background pure white. Title in dark grey #333333, all capitals, widely letter-spaced, geometric sans-serif (Gotham Medium style), about 1.7% of slide height with caps. Section headings in near-black #111111, Gotham Book style sans-serif regular weight, about 1.8% of slide height. Body text in mid grey #6E6E6E, Gotham Book sans-serif regular, about 1.8% of slide height, with generous line spacing (about 2.0% line pitch). Chart titles in grey #7E7E7E, Gotham Medium, about 1.8%. Chart axis labels in #595959 Gotham Book, about 1.35%. Accent colours navy #1F497D for bars and red #C0504D for the highlighted 2022 bars. Divider rules are thin light grey lines about #BFBFBF, roughly 1 pt. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients. Page number in light grey #BABABA, Gotham Medium, about 1.6%.

### Slide 12

**Text**

OUTLOOK
Volume
We are planning to grow production as quickly as possible in alignment with the 50% CAGR target we began guiding / to in early 2021.  In some years we may grow faster and some we may grow slower, depending on a number of factors. / For 2023, we expect to remain ahead of the long-term 50% CAGR with around 1.8M cars for the year.
Cash
We have sufficient liquidity to fund our product roadmap, long-term capacity expansion plans and other expenses. / Furthermore, we will manage the business such that we maintain a strong balance sheet during this uncertain period.
Profit
While we continue to execute on innovations to reduce the cost of manufacturing and operations, over time, we / expect our hardware-related profits to be accompanied with an acceleration of software-related profits. We continue / to believe that our operating margin will remain the highest among volume OEMs.
Product
Cybertruck remains on track to begin production later this year at Gigafactory Texas. Our next generation vehicle / platform is under development, with additional details to be shared at Investor Day (March 1st 2023).
12
T E S L A

**Layout**

Single-column text slide on a white field with two columns: short bold-weight row labels in a narrow left column starting at about 4.5% of slide width, and body paragraphs in a wider right column starting at about 18.6% of slide width. The section heading OUTLOOK sits top-left at about 9.3% from the top, aligned to the same left margin as the row labels. Four stacked rows are spaced unevenly down the slide, ending near 72% of the height, leaving the lower area empty. Page number 12 sits bottom-left at about 95% from the top and a faint TESLA wordmark sits bottom-right on the same baseline band; margins are about 4.5% left.

**Visuals**

none

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradients, shadows, boxes, rules or shapes. Row labels (Volume, Cash, Profit, Product) are book-weight geometric sans-serif in near-black #111111. Body paragraphs are book-weight geometric sans-serif in mid grey #6E6E6E with generous letter spacing and about 1.79% of slide height type size, line pitch about 2.6% of slide height. Heading is about 1.74% of slide height. Page number is medium weight in light grey #BABABA. Wordmark is very pale grey, about #D0D0D0. The typeface is GothamSSm (Gotham-style geometric sans-serif).

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: Execution is disciplined, with consistent small-caps section labels, a shaded Q4 column that guides the eye in tables, and strong full-bleed factory photos on slides 14 to 21.

**Text**

GIGAFACTORY BERLIN IS A PIECE OF ART
14
forch.load
(factoryintheforest.pt)
088 089 090 097 098 099
T E S L A

**Layout**

A single full-width photograph sits in a box spanning nearly the whole slide width, from about 4.5% to 95.5% horizontally and 12.1% to 93% vertically. The headline sits above the photo at top left, left-aligned with the photo's left edge at about 4.6%, on one baseline at about 9.3% from the top. The page number sits bottom left at about 3% and the Tesla wordmark sits bottom right, both below the photo, with no footer text and no other furniture.

**Visuals**

One photograph, boxed with hard rectangular edges and not cropped to a shape, full-bleed or overlaid. It shows the loading-dock side of the Gigafactory Berlin at dusk. On the left, a tall dark wall about one-third of the image width carries a large printed mural of a green and magenta pixelated forest, with a pale pink rectangular cutout panel and small white monospaced text reading forch.load and (factoryintheforest.pt). In the centre and right, a row of semi-trailers is parked nose-to-tail at loading bays, with side panels in blue, dark green, orange-brown, white and grey; a green-and-orange trailer and a white trailer are prominent in the foreground. Concrete bay walls carry black dock numbers 088, 089, 090, 097, 098 and 099 on white plates. Several concrete towers behind the docks are painted with abstract, glossy, multicoloured geometric murals in blue, magenta, violet, green and white. A grey clad building with a horizontal band of windows runs behind. On the right, a wide empty tarmac apron with a yellow-and-white dashed crossing, two white Mercedes-style vans, a small red vehicle, a pedestrian in dark clothing, and a road with cars and white lorries; a row of light poles and a tree line sit on the horizon. A small white moon is visible in the pale sky. Lighting is soft, even dusk daylight with a pale lavender-to-pink gradient sky. Dominant colours are dark teal, emerald green, magenta, violet, slate grey, concrete grey and off-white. Composition is a wide landscape frame with the mural wall as a vertical anchor at left and the receding dock line leading the eye to the right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide background #FFFFFF with no gradient or texture. The headline is set in a geometric sans-serif such as Gotham Medium, all capitals, widely letter-spaced, in two colour runs: GIGAFACTORY BERLIN in dark grey #333333 and IS A PIECE OF ART in mid grey #888888. The page number is in the same sans-serif in light grey #BABABA. The Tesla wordmark at bottom right is in very pale grey, widely spaced capitals. Headline cap height is roughly 1.7% of slide height. The photo has square corners, no border, no shadow and no overlay.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

**Text**

GIGAFACTORY BERLIN — MODEL Y PAINT SHOP / 15

**Layout**

Single full-width photograph below a small letter-spaced header line at top left, with the page number bottom left and a faint Tesla wordmark bottom right. Header text starts at about x 4.6% and the photo spans about x 4.5% to 95.5% and y 12.1% to 92.9%, giving roughly 4.5% side margins. There is no footer text beyond the page number and wordmark.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a horizontal framed image of a paint booth at the Gigafactory Berlin Model Y paint shop. Subject: an unpainted, light grey primed body shell of a Model Y, seen head-on from the front and centred on a carrier with a dark charcoal underframe. Three to four painting robots covered in white protective plastic sheeting reach toward the body from the left and right, with spray nozzles at the ends of their arms; a fine white paint mist hangs in the air around the body. Composition: symmetrical one-point view down the booth, the car centred, the robots framing both sides, the back wall of the booth blurred. Lighting: even, soft, high-key diffuse light with no hard shadows. Dominant colours: white, pale grey, light silver and charcoal black on the car underframe and grated dark grey floor. Floor shows a dark metal grating and yellow-and-black hazard stripe marking at the left; grey concrete blocks sit on the floor beside the robots. The photo is not cropped to a shape, is boxed inside the margins (not full-bleed), and has no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no gradient or pattern. Header text is widely letter-spaced uppercase geometric sans-serif (Gotham Medium style), with the first half in dark grey #333333 and the second half in mid grey #888888. Page number in light grey #BABABA. Photograph is a rectangle with square corners, no border and no shadow. Title size is about 1.74% of slide height and the page number about 1.58%. No body text.

One photograph in a rectangular box, not cropped to a shape and not full-bleed, with a white margin around it. Each enclosure has a white roof with five-plus circular fan grilles and a ventilated louvre band, and a red TESLA wordmark on the right end of the roof. The front face shows about ten vertical battery racks, each holding stacked orange battery modules in three tiers, with one central cabinet containing an inverter and a black breaker box and an open service bay. Behind is a busy factory floor with workers in hi-vis vests, shelving, red tool carts, white racks of cabinets, cardboard boxes, a grey IBC tote at upper right, a yellow forklift at right edge, and chain-link fencing with a small monitor on a post at right. Small white serial-number labels lie on the grey concrete floor in front. Composition: elevated, centred, the battery unit fills the lower two-thirds of the photo. Lighting: even bright overhead factory light, no strong shadows. Dominant colours: white, orange, mid grey concrete, with grey steel columns. No overlay or tint.

Subject: a long white press housing with the large grey TESLA wordmark (letters T, E, S, L, A) across its upper face and a red horizontal stripe running diagonally along its top edge. Several small warning labels are on the white carriages. A yellow robotic arm is partly visible at the right beside the press. Action: the tooling sets are staged on the floor in a line receding into the distance, not in motion. Framing: wide, low-angle, eye-level to slightly below, straight-on horizontal composition, full subject visible from left to right with the press running diagonally. Lighting: bright, even overhead industrial lighting from ceiling fixtures and skylights, soft shadows on the press face. Dominant colours: white and light grey (press and ceiling), mid grey (TESLA letters, floor), saturated blue (tooling frames), red (stripe), small yellow accents. Not cropped; boxed within the margins, not full-bleed; no overlay or tint. Also a faint grey Tesla wordmark logo at bottom right.

**Text**

GIGAFACTORY SHANGHAI — BODY SHOP
18
TESLA

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a letter-spaced title line in the top-left above a full-width photograph that spans nearly the whole slide width, with a left margin of about 4.5% and a right margin of about 4.5%. The page number sits bottom-left below the photo and a faint Tesla wordmark sits bottom-right; no footer text or slide date.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a full-width rectangular image (not cropped to a shape, not full-bleed, no border or shadow) occupying about x 4.5%, y 12.1%, w 91.0%, h 80.8% of the slide, as a 4396 by 2198 pixel image. Subject: a car body-in-white on a production line at the Tesla Gigafactory Shanghai body shop, photographed from an elevated viewpoint looking down the line in a symmetrical, centred composition. Eight to ten orange KUKA industrial robot arms with black hoses and black tool heads reach in from the left and right and from the back, several holding metal clamping fixtures and welding tools over the silver-white car body in the centre. The body is bare white/silver steel with a red-orange fixture frame beneath it and a red jig platform in the foreground centre. Black cylindrical robot pedestals stand around the cell, and grey-glass safety cages with metal mesh line the walls and catwalks. A yellow label with black print reads approximately FRM2 C40 P271 on a robot base at left, and a red-and-white prohibition sign is on the floor at lower left. Lighting is bright, even, cool overhead factory light with soft reflections on the steel. Dominant colours: vivid orange-red #E0401E on the robots, silver-white #D9DDE0 on the body, black #1A1A1A on pedestals and hoses, pale teal-grey #8FB3B8 in the background cages and walls. Overlay or tint: none; the photo is unaltered. Right-side and foreground areas are slightly soft in focus.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal white layout. Title line is set in widely letter-spaced geometric sans-serif capitals (Gotham SSm Medium style), dark grey #333333 for GIGAFACTORY SHANGHAI — and mid grey #888888 for BODY SHOP. Page number in light grey #BABABA. Tesla wordmark in very pale grey #BABABA-like tone, letter-spaced. No shapes, rules, gradients or corner radii; the only graphic element is the photograph.

GIGAFACTORY NEVADA — EXPANSION PLANS
19
T E S L A

**Layout**

Single-image slide with a small letter-spaced title at top left above a wide photograph that spans nearly the full width inside the margins, with a page number bottom left and a faint wordmark bottom right. The title sits at about 9.3% from the top and the image runs from about 12.1% to 92.9% of the height, left edge about 4.5% and right edge about 95.5%. The page number sits on the left margin at about 95% height and the wordmark on the right margin at the same baseline.

**Visuals**

One photograph, a photorealistic aerial rendering of the Tesla Gigafactory Nevada. A very long, low, white-framed factory building runs diagonally across the centre-left of the frame, its roof covered in dark rectangular solar panels with large white TESLA letters set across the roof. The lower front façade is dark glass with a small 'TESLA' sign at its centre. A paved plaza with trees and a dark reflecting pool sits in front of the entrance. Along the right side, a loading dock has a row of white semi-trailers parked end to end, and beyond it large car parks are filled with rows of cars, many with red bodies. Trucks and small vehicles move on the access roads in the foreground and at left. Behind the building are tan and beige desert hills with sparse scrub, under a pale blue sky with white cumulus clouds near the horizon. Lighting is soft daylight from the upper left, with the building's roof lit warm and shadows falling to the right. Dominant colours are beige and tan desert, dark grey asphalt and solar panels, white building trim, and pale blue sky. The photograph is a full-colour rectangle, not cropped, not full-bleed, sitting inside the page margins with square corners and no overlay or tint. Wide framing taken from an elevated three-quarter aerial viewpoint.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no decoration. The title is set in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham SSm Medium style) in all caps with very wide letter-spacing. The first part, GIGAFACTORY NEVADA —, is dark grey #333333 and the second part, EXPANSION PLANS, is mid grey #888888. Title cap height is roughly 1.7% of slide height. The page number is light grey #BABABA in the same family at about 1.6% of slide height. The wordmark is a very pale grey letter-spaced sans logo. The photograph has square corners and no border or shadow.

**Layout**

Three equal-width bar charts sit side by side in a three-column arrangement across the middle of a white slide, each with its own y-axis at its left edge and category labels rotated beneath it. The header block KEY METRICS QUARTERLY with (Unaudited) sits top-left at the left margin, and the page number 22 sits bottom-left with a faint TESLA wordmark bottom-right; there is no footer text, and the slide number is bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Three vertical clustered bar charts with thin light-grey horizontal gridlines and no chart borders. Chart 1 (left): Vehicle Deliveries (millions of units), single blue series, y-axis 0.0 to 0.5 in steps of 0.1, 12 categories 1Q-2020 to 4Q-2022 with values about 0.088, 0.090, 0.139, 0.180, 0.184, 0.201, 0.241, 0.308, 0.310, 0.255, 0.343, 0.405 (no printed values; estimates). No legend; axis title is the caption below. Chart 2 (middle): clustered columns, y-axis -1 to 6 in steps of 1, 12 categories 1Q-2020 to 4Q-2022. **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no decoration. Title KEY METRICS QUARTERLY is set in widely letter-spaced uppercase, with KEY METRICS in dark grey #333333 and QUARTERLY in mid grey #888888, a medium-weight geometric sans-serif (Gotham-like) at about 1.7% of slide height. **Visuals**

Three clustered column charts on a white background, all with 12 quarters from 1Q-2020 to 4Q-2022 on the horizontal axis, labels rotated 90 degrees. Operating Cash Flow series in blue, about values 2.6, 2.7, 4.3, 5.9, 8.0, 9.1, 9.9, 11.5, 13.8, 14.0, 16.0, 14.7. Free Cash Flow series in red, about values 1.1, 0.9, 1.9, 2.7, 3.9, 4.1, 4.0, 4.9, 6.9, 6.9, 8.9, 7.5. Legend below the chart: blue square Operating Cash Flow ($B), red square Free Cash Flow ($B). Chart 3, Net Income ($B) and Adjusted EBITDA ($B): y-axis -3 to 21 with gridlines at 3 intervals and the zero line visible; Net Income in blue, about values -0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.1, 2.1, 3.4, 5.5, 8.4, 9.5, 11.1, 12.5; Adjusted EBITDA in red, about values 3.7, 4.4, 5.0, 5.7, 6.6, 8.0, 9.3, 11.6, 14.7, 16.1, 17.8, 19.2. ### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.
- What would change the score: I would add direct data labels and takeaways to the key metrics charts on slides 22 to 24, which have tiny rotated axis labels and no callouts.

Source: OEM financial disclosures, Bloomberg
Autos Industry includes: Tesla, BMW, Mercedes-Benz, Ford, GM, Honda, Hyundai, Nissan, Toyota and VW. Stellantis is excluded given limited historical disclosures due to the recent merger between FCA and PSA.
Autos Industry operating margin is calculated by dividing the sum of USD equivalent operating profits for the entire industry by the USD equivalent revenues for respective periods.

**Layout**

Two equal-width line charts sit side by side in two columns below a left-aligned header block; each chart has a centred title above its plot and a legend beneath its x-axis. The header starts at about 4.6% from the left edge at about 9% of the slide height. The footer runs along the bottom-left with the page number 24 at the far left margin, source lines to its right, and a faint Tesla wordmark at the bottom-right corner. No slide title is set apart from the header; the page has no border and no logo other than the footer wordmark.

**Visuals**

Two line charts with no markers, no data labels, and a solid black horizontal zero line in each. Left chart: title 'YoY Revenue Growth'; y-axis from -20% to 90% in 10% steps with light grey horizontal gridlines; x-axis categories Q1-2019 to Q4-2022 (16 quarters) rotated vertical; no axis titles. Series: Tesla red line, about 81%, 82%, 39%, 14%, 15%, 3%, 16%, 28%, 38%, 63%, 66%, 71%, 73%, 60%, 60%, 51% (ends at Q4-2022); Autos Industry grey line, about 1%, 0%, 0%, -1%, -2%, -12%, -14%, -12%, -7%, 15%, 17%, 13%, 9%, -3%, -1% (ends at Q3-2022); S&P 500 blue line, about 8%, 6%, 5%, 6%, 4%, 0%, -2%, -4%, -1%, 6%, 10%, 13%, 15%, 15%, 14% (ends at Q3-2022). Legend below the x-axis: red Tesla, grey Autos Industry, blue S&P 500. Right chart: title 'Operating Margin'; y-axis from -4% to 18% in 2% steps; same 16 quarter categories rotated vertical. Series: Tesla red line, about -1.4%, 0.6%, -0.1%, -0.3%, 2.8%, 4.8%, 6.3%, 6.3%, 6.4%, 7.7%, 9.6%, 12.2%, 15.3%, 16.0%, 16.6%, 16.8% (ends at Q4-2022); Autos Industry grey line, about 5.1%, 4.8%, 4.9%, 4.7%, 4.1%, 2.4%, 2.6%, 3.7%, 5.5%, 8.1%, 8.2%, 8.2%, 8.4%, 8.0%, 8.0% (ends at Q3-2022); S&P 500 blue line, about 13.0%, 12.9%, 12.8%, 13.2%, 11.4%, 10.3%, 10.0%, 9.4%, 11.5%, 13.2%, 14.0%, 15.2%, 15.2%, 14.7%, 14.3% (ends at Q3-2022). Legend below: red Tesla, grey Autos Industry, blue S&P 500. No highlighted points. Lines are thick, about 3 pt equivalent, with round joins.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no gradients, shadows or boxes. Header 'KEY METRICS' in Gotham SSm Medium caps, colour #333333, wide letter-spacing; 'TRAILING 12 MONTHS (TTM)' in the same family, Medium, caps, #888888, wide letter-spacing; '(Unaudited)' in Medium, #888888, sentence case. Title size about 1.74% of slide height, which is about 0.7 of... the cap height of the header is roughly 1.2% of slide height. Chart titles in Gotham SSm Book, #7F7F7F, about 1.79% of slide height. Axis tick labels and legend in Gotham SSm Book, #595959, about 1.79% of slide height. Footer and source text in Gotham SSm Book, #BFBFBF, about 1.35% of slide height; page number in Gotham SSm Medium, #BABABA, about 1.58%. Series colours: Tesla #C00000 red, Autos Industry #A6A6A6 grey, S&P 500 #4F81BD blue. Gridlines #D9D9D9 hairline; zero lines #000000 hairline. Body text is set in sans-serif, with no italics.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Plots ASP against operating margin on a shared time axis, making the trade-off between price and profitability visible in one chart.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.
- Worth keeping: Keep the shaded current-period column in the tables (slides 4, 5, 7, 8), the dual-axis ASP versus margin chart on slide 25, the large clean photo pages, and the agenda with page numbers.

**Text**

¹ Vehicle ASP is calculated by taking automotive sales (excluding regulatory credits and automotive leasing) and dividing by deliveries (adjusted for operating and sales-type leases) for each respective period.
T E S L A

**Layout**

Title block sits top-left at about 4.6% from the left edge and 9.3% from the top, with the subtitle '(Unaudited)' beneath it. A single combined chart fills the middle of the slide between about x 4.4% and 89.3%, with its chart title centred above the plot, a dual value axis on the left and right, year labels under the bars and a centred legend below. The footnote runs along the bottom at about 93.3% from the top, with the page number bottom-left at x 3.0% and a faint TESLA wordmark bottom-right; all text is left-aligned to the title margin of about 4.6%.

**Visuals**

Combined bar and line chart. Chart title: 'Vehicle ASP¹ vs. Operating Margin (GAAP)'. Left axis: Vehicle ASP in US dollars, $0 to $120,000 in steps of $20,000, gridlines between. Right axis: Operating Margin in percent, -18% to 18% in steps of 6%, aligned to the same gridlines. Years 2017 to 2022 on the category axis, no axis title. Bar series 'ASP' (blue gradient bars, six bars): about $106,700 (2017), about $73,400 (2018), about $56,500 (2019), about $53,100 (2020), about $50,900 (2021), about $53,700 (2022). Line series 'Operating Margin' (red line with a marker-like round end at each year, no printed values): about -14% (2017), about -2% (2018), about 0% (2019), about 6% (2020), about 12% (2021), about 17% (2022). Estimates are read from the plot against the axes. Legend centred below the years: blue square labelled ASP, red line labelled Operating Margin. No highlighted points and no data labels printed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a minimal, flat, Tesla-style corporate look. Title 'KEY METRICS' in dark grey #333333 and 'ASP VS. OPERATING MARGINS' in mid grey #888888, both in widely letter-spaced medium-weight geometric sans-serif capitals about 1.74% of slide height. Subtitle '(Unaudited)' in grey #888888, sentence case, same size. Chart title and axis labels in grey (#7F7F7F to #595959) light-weight geometric sans-serif about 1.79% of slide height, which is small relative to the 2% title size. Bars: blue gradient from dark navy #2A4D7F at top-left to lighter blue #4580C6 at bottom-right. Operating margin line: red #C00000, about 0.45% of slide height thick, round caps and joins. Gridlines hairline light grey #D9D9D9. No shadows, no corner radii, no gradients other than the bars.

### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Overlays reported deliveries against a 50% CAGR reference line, turning a forward target into a visual the audience can check.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slide 3 is a dense wall of small grey-on-grey text with low contrast, titles are label-only so the takeaway is never stated (for example slides 22 to 26 and 9), and the narrative on slides 6, 10 and 11 is small paragraph text beside charts.

**Text**

KEY METRICS VOLUME GROWTH / (Unaudited) / Vehicle Delivery Growth Rate – Assuming 50% CAGR Since 2020 / 1,800,000 / 1,600,000 / 1,400,000 / 1,200,000 / 1,000,000 / 800,000 / 600,000 / 400,000 / 200,000 / 0 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 50% CAGR Since 2020 / Reported Deliveries / Note: We expect to achieve 50% average annual growth in vehicle production and deliveries over a multi-year horizon, although not necessarily on a yearly basis. / 26

**Layout**

Single chart slide on a white field. A small all-caps section label with its '(Unaudited)' qualifier sits top-left at about 4.6% from the left edge and 9.3% from the top; the chart title is centred above the plot area at about 18.6% from the top. The plot spans about 11% to 92.5% horizontally and 23% to 76% vertically, with four year groups (2020 to 2023) on the category axis and a legend centred below the year labels. The footnote runs along the bottom at about 93% from the top, left-aligned at 4.6%, with the page number at bottom-left (3%) and a faint TESLA wordmark at bottom-right (about 92% to 97%).

**Visuals**

Chart: clustered vertical bar chart. Chart title: 'Vehicle Delivery Growth Rate – Assuming 50% CAGR Since 2020' (no unit stated). Y-axis: 0 to 1,800,000 (vehicles), gridlines every 200,000, labels printed as full numbers with thousands commas. X-axis: years 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023. Series 1, '50% CAGR Since 2020' (blue bars, left of each pair): slide prints no values, so about 500,000 (2020), about 750,000 (2021), about 1,125,000 (2022), about 1,685,000 (2023). Series 2, 'Reported Deliveries' (red bars, right of each pair): no values printed, so about 500,000 (2020), about 935,000 (2021), about 1,315,000 (2022); no 2023 red bar is shown. Legend: centred below the x-axis, blue square followed by '50% CAGR Since 2020', red square followed by 'Reported Deliveries'. No highlighted points. Bars have a soft radial/vertical gradient: blue bars shade from dark steel blue at the edges to lighter blue at the centre, red bars shade from dark maroon at the edges to lighter red at the centre. Bar tops are flat with very small rounded corners.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal corporate look. Background pure white. Section label in widely letter-spaced geometric sans-serif capitals (Gotham-style, Medium weight); the first word dark grey #333333 and the second word mid grey #888888; the '(Unaudited)' line in regular-weight grey #888888. Chart title and axis labels in a light-to-book geometric sans-serif (Gotham Book style), grey #7F7F7F for the chart title and #595959 for axis and legend labels. Title text about 1.7% of slide height, axis and legend labels about 1.8%, footnote about 1.35% in very light grey #BFBFBF, page number about 1.6% in #BABABA Medium. Accents: blue #3F6FA8 and red #A8403F for the series. Gridlines are hairline light grey (#D9D9D9), baseline slightly darker. No shadows, borders or corner radii beyond the minimal rounding on bars.

### Slide 27

**Text**

FINANCIAL STATEMENTS

**Layout**

Single centred title on an otherwise empty dark slide; the title sits on the horizontal and vertical centre line with wide letter-spacing. No footer, no slide number; a small TESLA wordmark sits in the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

The only non-text element is a small grey TESLA wordmark in the bottom-right corner, set in widely spaced capitals with a stylised letterform design (the T, E, S, L, A drawn in a geometric, squared style). No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid near-black #111111 fill covering the entire slide. Full-bleed near-black background #111111. Title in white #FFFFFF, Gotham-style geometric sans-serif (GothamSSm Medium), all capitals, very wide letter-spacing (about 0.3 em), single line, size about 2.4% of slide height. No body text. Wordmark in mid-grey #8A8A8A, small, about 1% of slide height. No shapes, lines, shadows or gradients.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate financial table on white. Section labels and total rows sit on light grey bands (#F2F2F2). The Q4-2022 column is shaded pale pink (#F2DCDB approx.). Page number '28' is GothamSSm-Medium #BABABA at about 1.58%. Thin light grey horizontal rules separate rows, and there are no shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders around the table.

**Layout**

Single full-width table on a white slide with the title block top-left at about 4.6% from the left edge and 9.4% from the top, and the table running from about 4.6% to 95% of the width beneath it. Row labels are left-aligned in the first column, numbers are right-aligned under the year headers 2018 to 2022, with the 2022 column set apart as a tinted highlight. The page number 32 sits bottom-left at about 3% from the left, the footnote runs along the bottom beside it, and a faint Tesla wordmark sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A data table with a header row and 22 body rows, in USD millions except per-share and percentage rows. Columns: row label, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022. Rows, in order: In millions of USD or shares as applicable, except per share data (header); Net (loss) income attributable to common stockholders (GAAP): (976), (862), 721, 5,519, 12,556; Stock-based compensation expense: 749, 898, 1,734, 2,121, 1,560; Net (loss) income attributable to common stockholders (non-GAAP): (227), 36, 2,455, 7,640, 14,116; Less: Buy-out of noncontrolling interest: —, 8, 31, (5), (27); Less: Dilutive convertible debt: —, —, —, (9), (1); Net (loss) income used in computing diluted EPS attributable to common stockholders (non-GAAP): (227), 28, 2,424, 7,654, 14,144; EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (GAAP)(1): (0.38), (0.33), 0.21, 1.63, 3.62; Stock-based compensation expense per share(1): 0.29, 0.34, 0.54, 0.63, 0.45; EPS attributable to common stockholders, diluted (non-GAAP)(1): (0.09), 0.01, 0.75, 2.26, 4.07; Shares used in EPS calculation, diluted (GAAP and non-GAAP)(1): 2,558, 2,661, 3,249, 3,386, 3,475; Net (loss) income attributable to common stockholders (GAAP): (976), (862), 721, 5,519, 12,556; Interest expense: 663, 685, 748, 371, 191; Provision for income taxes: 58, 110, 292, 699, 1,132; Depreciation, amortization and impairment: 1,901, 2,154, 2,322, 2,911, 3,747; Stock-based compensation expense: 749, 898, 1,734, 2,121, 1,560; Adjusted EBITDA (non-GAAP): 2,395, 2,985, 5,817, 11,621, 19,186; Total revenues: 21,461, 24,578, 31,536, 53,823, 81,462; Adjusted EBITDA margin (non-GAAP): 11.2%, 12.1%, 18.4%, 21.6%, 23.6%; Automotive gross margin (GAAP): 23.4%, 21.2%, 25.6%, 29.3%, 28.5%; Less: Total regulatory credit revenue recognized: 1.7%, 2.3%, 4.6%, 2.3%, 1.8%; Automotive gross margin excluding regulatory credit sales (non-GAAP): 21.7%, 18.9%, 21.0%, 27.0%, 26.7%. Rows are separated by thin light-grey hairlines. Four full-width-of-label light grey bands (#F2F2F2) sit behind the header row and the three section separators. The 2022 column has a pale pink tint (about #F2DCDC) behind its cells, which are set in the same weight as the rest. Bold weight is used for the GAAP and non-GAAP subtotal labels and their values. Superscript (1) markers follow the labels for rows that refer to the footnote. No photographs, icons or charts are present; the only graphic element besides the table is the faint Tesla wordmark at bottom right, rendered as light grey spaced capitals T E S L A with a small mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient, border or shadow. Title is set in letter-spaced uppercase in GothamSSm-Medium at about 1.74% of slide height, colour #333333. Table body text is GothamSSm-Book at about 1.46% of slide height, colour #000000, with bold weight for subtotal labels. Accent is a pale pink tint (about #F2DCDC) on the 2022 column and light grey bands #F2F2F2. Sans-serif geometric typeface, closest to Gotham. Footnote and page number are very light grey (#BFBFBF and #BABABA). Rules and bands are flat with square corners and no shadow.

### Slide 34

**Text**

ADDITIONAL INFORMATION / WEBCAST INFORMATION / Tesla will provide a live webcast of its fourth quarter 2022 financial results conference call beginning at 4:30 p.m. CT on January 25, 2023 at ir.tesla.com. This webcast will also be available for replay for approximately one year thereafter. / CERTAIN TERMS / When used in this update, certain terms have the following meanings. Our vehicle deliveries include only vehicles that have been transferred to end customers with all paperwork correctly completed. Our energy product deployment volume includes both customer units installed and equipment sales; we report installations at time of commissioning for storage projects or inspection for solar projects, and equipment sales at time of delivery. "Adjusted EBITDA" is equal to (i) net income (loss) attributable to common stockholders before (ii)(a) interest expense, (b) provision for income taxes, (c) depreciation, amortization and impairment and (d) stock-based compensation expense, which is the same measurement for this term pursuant to the performance-based stock option award granted to our CEO in 2018. "Free cash flow" is operating cash flow less capital expenditures. Average cost per vehicle is cost of automotive sales divided by new vehicle deliveries (excluding leases). “Days sales outstanding” is equal to (i) average accounts receivable, net for the period divided by (ii) total revenues and multiplied by (iii) the number of days in the period. “Days payable outstanding” is equal to (i) average accounts payable for the period divided by (ii) total cost of revenues and multiplied by (iii) the number of days in the period. “Days of supply” is calculated by dividing new car ending inventory by the relevant quarter’s deliveries and using 75 trading days. Constant currency impacts are calculated by comparing actuals against current results converted into USD using average exchange rates from the prior period. / NON-GAAP FINANCIAL INFORMATION / Consolidated financial information has been presented in accordance with GAAP as well as on a non-GAAP basis to supplement our consolidated financial results. Our non-GAAP financial measures include non-GAAP automotive gross margin, non-GAAP net income (loss) attributable to common stockholders, non-GAAP net income (loss) attributable to common stockholders on a diluted per share basis (calculated using weighted average shares for GAAP diluted net income (loss) attributable to common stockholders), Adjusted EBITDA, Adjusted EBITDA margin and free cash flow. These non-GAAP financial measures also facilitate management’s internal comparisons to Tesla’s historical performance as well as comparisons to the operating results of other companies. Management believes that it is useful to supplement its GAAP financial statements with this non-GAAP information because management uses such information internally for its operating, budgeting and financial planning purposes. Management also believes that presentation of the non-GAAP financial measures provides useful information to our investors regarding our financial condition and results of operations, so that investors can see through the eyes of Tesla management regarding important financial metrics that Tesla uses to run the business and allowing investors to better understand Tesla’s performance. Non-GAAP information is not prepared under a comprehensive set of accounting rules and therefore, should only be read in conjunction with financial information reported under U.S. GAAP when understanding Tesla’s operating performance. A reconciliation between GAAP and non-GAAP financial information is provided above. / FORWARD-LOOKING STATEMENTS / Certain statements in this update, including statements in the “Outlook” section; statements relating to the future development, ramp, production capacity and output rates, supply chain, demand and market growth, cost, pricing and profitability, deliveries, deployment, availability and other features and improvements and timing of existing and future Tesla products and technologies such as Model 3, Model Y, Model X, Model S, Cybertruck, Tesla Semi, our next generation vehicle platform, our Autopilot, Full Self-Driving and other vehicle software and our energy storage and solar products; statements regarding operating margin, operating profits, spending and liquidity; and statements regarding expansion, improvements and/or ramp and related timing at the California Factory, Gigafactory Shanghai, Gigafactory Berlin-Brandenburg, Gigafactory Texas and Megapack factory are “forward-looking statements” that are subject to risks and uncertainties. These forward-looking statements are based on management’s current expectations, and as a result of certain risks and uncertainties, actual results may differ materially from those projected. The following important factors, without limitation, could cause actual results to differ materially from those in the forward-looking statements: uncertainties in future macroeconomic and regulatory conditions arising from the current global pandemic; the risk of delays in launching and manufacturing our products and features cost-effectively; our ability to grow our sales, delivery, installation, servicing and charging capabilities and effectively manage this growth; consumers’ demand for electric vehicles generally and our vehicles specifically; the ability of suppliers to deliver components according to schedules, prices, quality and volumes acceptable to us, and our ability to manage such components effectively; any issues with lithium-ion cells or other components manufactured at Gigafactory Nevada and Gigafactory Shanghai; our ability to ramp Gigafactory Shanghai, Gigafactory Berlin-Brandenburg and Gigafactory Texas in accordance with our plans; our ability to procure supply of battery cells, including through our own manufacturing; risks relating to international expansion; any failures by Tesla products to perform as expected or if product recalls occur; the risk of product liability claims; competition in the automotive and energy product markets; our ability to maintain public credibility and confidence in our long-term business prospects; our ability to manage risks relating to our various product financing programs; the status of government and economic incentives for electric vehicles and energy products; our ability to attract, hire and retain key employees and qualified personnel and ramp our installation teams; our ability to maintain the security of our information and production and product systems; our compliance with various regulations and laws applicable to our operations and products, which may evolve from time to time; risks relating to our indebtedness and financing strategies; and adverse foreign exchange movements. More information on potential factors that could affect our financial results is included from time to time in our Securities and Exchange Commission filings and reports, including the risks identified under the section captioned “Risk Factors” in our quarterly report on Form 10-Q filed with the SEC on October 24, 2022. Tesla disclaims any obligation to update information contained in these forward-looking statements whether as a result of new information, future events or otherwise. / 34 / TESLA

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text column running the full width of the page between a left margin of about 4.6% and a right margin of about 4.6%, with the letter-spaced title ADDITIONAL INFORMATION at the top left (y 9.4%) and four underlined section headings stacked above justified body paragraphs. The page number 34 sits at the bottom left (x 3.0%, y 95.1%) and a small Tesla wordmark sits at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

None apart from the small light-grey Tesla wordmark logo at the bottom right, set with wide letter spacing.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title ADDITIONAL INFORMATION in GothamSSm-Medium, #333333, widely letter-spaced capitals, about 1.74% of slide height. Section headings WEBCAST INFORMATION, CERTAIN TERMS, NON-GAAP FINANCIAL INFORMATION and FORWARD-LOOKING STATEMENTS in GothamSSm-Book, #000000, all capitals, underlined, about 1.35% of slide height. Body text in GothamSSm-Book, #000000, about 1.35% of slide height, justified, with generous line spacing of about 1.6 and paragraph gaps of about one line. Page number and wordmark in light grey (#BABABA and about #C8C8C8). Geometric sans-serif throughout. No shapes, rules, shadows or gradients.
